# Skills · T · part 6

1,024 entries, tda-resource-preflight to tdk-repo-worktree. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tda-resource-preflight**: Use before launching compute that may exceed ~30 minutes — bootstraps, permutation nulls, Markov batteries, MICE refits, per-cluster or per-individual batteries, PH at high landmark count, or large ETL / memory-sensitive dataframe work. ([ZK-Theory/TDL](https://github.com/ZK-Theory/TDL/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/tda-resource-preflight) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **tda-scenario-stress-test**: Use when a TDL decision has multiple plausible branches — paper structure, adding a robustness check or baseline, an acceleration strategy, a methods trade-off — and needs structured risk analysis before it becomes a task brief. - **tda-statistical-analysis-review**: Use when reviewing statistical output headed for a paper — permutation tests, Markov memory-ladder results, confidence intervals, effect sizes, model fits, or statistical claims in P01-A/P01-B/P04 — after the numbers exist and before prose depends on them. ([ZK-Theory/TDL](https://github.com/ZK-Theory/TDL/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/tda-statistical-analysis-review) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **tda-statistical-modeling-toolkit**: Use when fitting or reviewing non-topological statistical models in TDL — GLMs, OLS/logit/probit, survival models, clustered models, bootstrap summaries, Bayesian robustness checks, simulation-based sensitivity checks, or power analysis — as baselines, robustness checks, or empirical context. - **tda-trajectory-baselines**: Use when building or reviewing conventional non-topological baselines for trajectory work — clustering, classification, forecasting, anomaly detection, or survival/duration comparison — to contextualize a persistent-homology result.
